The order creates a Board of Inquiry under section 206 of the Labor Management Relations Act, 1947 to inquire into the issues in a labor dispute affecting the copper and non-ferrous metals industry, with a report due on a stated date and a provision that, once the report is submitted, the Board continues in existence to perform other functions required under the Act until the President terminates it. Inventory: the order is one operative unit; the creation of the Board, its report date, and its continuation until terminated by the President are all within the single whole-order clause confirmed here. Ending: Executive Order 12553 of February 25, 1986 (51 FR 7237, held government printing) revokes the numbered orders in its table, and any amendments thereto; its No. Subject table lists 10283 with a subject line matching this order (creating a board of inquiry to report on a labor dispute). The enacting provision has no stated effective date, so the revocation is read as effective on signing, 1986-02-25; the Federal Register publication date 1986-03-03 and the filing date are not effective dates. The thirty-day or stated report date and the end of any statutory status-quo period are deadlines and spent facts, not ends (owner ruling 2026-10-21 on report deadlines): the order stayed on the books until express revocation. Searches: the OFR executive-order catalog search and the USC and eCFR citation searches (coverage notes read; no coverage warning) show no instrument other than EO 12553 revoking, superseding or amending this order; the held-text citation search found citations of this number only in printings of the order itself, orders printed on the same page, Federal Register index or codification-guide material and (for some) National Mediation Board report front matter.\nMember checks: {\"earlier_end_check\":\"Candidate earlier ends examined. (1) The report date stated in the order is a deadline for the Board, not an end. (2) The order itself provides that the Board continues until the Board is terminated by the President; a presidential termination would be an earlier express end, but no termination instrument for this Board appears in the held corpus or in the OFR catalog hits, so none is asserted. EO 12553 is itself a presidential revocation of the order by number and is the first held instrument ending it. (3) No later order amends or supersedes it in the held corpus. Evidence decides (owner ruling 2026-10-28): no earlier end is shown, so none is claimed.\",\"independent_command_check\":\"Independent commands: the continuation sentence (the Board continues in existence to perform other functions under the Act until terminated by the President) is part of the same board-creating order and shares its end; the powers and duties come from Title II of the Labor Management Relations Act, 1947, which the order cites and does not restate.\",\"savings_check\":\"Savings and residual effects: EO 12553's enacting provision contains no savings or exception clause (the ACT record for this pair lists none, and the held text has none). The board's work, report and any agreement reached are completed past events rather than continuing commands of this order; the order created no right, appointment or transfer that survives independently of it, and no held source shows any continuing independent effect.\",\"scope_check\":\"Scope: EO 12553 revokes the order by number and the held table row matches its subject; the enacting provision reaches any amendments thereto and has no exception, so it reaches the whole order, not part. The order has no amendment in the held corpus.\"}","review_dependency_issues":[],"reviews":[{"file_sha256":"6b067cb2b9d1ca895a51a7fa83f715f8f67dde108b6d8ee7dfd009452b533611","path":"research/excavator/claude-apollo-2026-10-31/g04/packet/complete-v1/receipts/research/excavator/claude-apollo-2026-10-31/g04/packet/reviews/BLIND_REVIEW.jsonl","pointer":"line:17","review_kind":"independent_ai_source_review","scope":"whole_case","verdict":"rejected","version_binding":"exact"},{"file_sha256":"6b067cb2b9d1ca895a51a7fa83f715f8f67dde108b6d8ee7dfd009452b533611","path":"research/excavator/claude-apollo-2026-10-31/g04/packet/reviews/BLIND_REVIEW.jsonl","pointer":"line:17","review_kind":"independent_ai_source_review","scope":"whole_case","verdict":"rejected","version_binding":"exact"}],"scopes":[{"aligned_current_scopes":["EO:10283#whole"],"alignment":"exact_quote_and_source","different_current_fields":["state_at_cutoff","first_end","later_events"],"scope_id":"EO:10283#whole"}],"source_pins":[{"held_text":{"expected_sha256":"6361bdc557b6acaeae6e6908cded18f4f8fef9fde1a97cc11fb7c12c8cdea761","path":"jobs/fr-originals/data/full_run/texts/EO_12553.txt","sha256":"6361bdc557b6acaeae6e6908cded18f4f8fef9fde1a97cc11fb7c12c8cdea761","state":"match"},"sha256":"945348afbba863ae7dc1fbe7df8759682fe1733962734a9255a1062c0561048c","text_path":"jobs/fr-originals/data/full_run/texts/EO_12553.txt","text_sha256":"6361bdc557b6acaeae6e6908cded18f4f8fef9fde1a97cc11fb7c12c8cdea761"},{"held_text":{"expected_sha256":"6bccec53f9356b34928279c169ebb1a551507bac9e370ffe661e5036d5db4a44","path":"jobs/fr-originals/data/full_run/texts/EO_10283.txt","sha256":"6bccec53f9356b34928279c169ebb1a551507bac9e370ffe661e5036d5db4a44","state":"match"},"sha256":"c88b12d10629646b8851d3af8f1bfcf791e7791825dd92b8e38eee23467d889a","text_path":"jobs/fr-originals/data/full_run/texts/EO_10283.txt","text_sha256":"6bccec53f9356b34928279c169ebb1a551507bac9e370ffe661e5036d5db4a44"}]}]}

The order creates a three-member emergency board under section 10 of the Railway Labor Act (subject to that section) to investigate the dispute between the Denver & Rio Grande Western Railroad Company, including the Denver & Salt Lake Railroad Company, a carrier under Federal management operated by the Secretary of the Army, and certain workers represented by the Brotherhood of Locomotive Engineers, requires the board to report within thirty days from the date of the order, a sentence that nothing in the order derogates from the authority of the Secretary of the Army under Executive Order 10155 of August 25, 1950, and a sentence requiring the Board to comply with section 502 of the Defense Production Act of 1950, as amended. Inventory: the order is one operative unit; the creation of the board, its thirty-day report deadline, the non-derogation sentence and the Defense Production Act compliance sentence are all within the single whole-order clause confirmed here. Ending: Executive Order 12553 of February 25, 1986 (51 FR 7237, held government printing) revokes the numbered orders in its table, and any amendments thereto; its No. Subject table lists 10285 with a subject line matching this order (creating an emergency board to investigate a labor dispute between certain workers and a railroad operated by the Secretary of the Army). The enacting provision has no stated effective date, so the revocation is read as effective on signing, 1986-02-25; the Federal Register publication date 1986-03-03 and the filing date are not effective dates. The thirty-day or stated report date and the end of any statutory status-quo period are deadlines and spent facts, not ends (owner ruling 2026-10-21 on report deadlines): the order stayed on the books until express revocation. Searches: the OFR executive-order catalog search and the USC and eCFR citation searches (coverage notes read; no coverage warning) show no instrument other than EO 12553 revoking, superseding or amending this order; the held-text citation search found citations of this number only in printings of the order itself, orders printed on the same page, Federal Register index or codification-guide material and (for some) National Mediation Board report front matter.\nMember checks: {\"earlier_end_check\":\"Candidate earlier ends examined. (1) The thirty-day report deadline is a deadline, not an end (owner ruling 2026-10-21): the order does not end when the board reports, nor when the statutory thirty-day status-quo period after the report runs out; actual report and standstill dates are spent facts that no reviewed event record in this packet supplies, so they are disclosed here rather than recorded as later events. (2) The statutory standstill in section 10 of the Railway Labor Act is the statute's own effect and not a separate command of this order. (3) No later order amends, supersedes or revokes the order before EO 12553 in the held corpus (OFR catalog and number-citation searches). EO 12553 is the first held instrument that revokes it. No earlier end is shown, so none is claimed. The sentence requiring compliance with section 502 of the Defense Production Act of 1950 is a condition on how the board performs its functions; the statute is not held in this repository, so no expiry of that statute is shown and none is claimed as an earlier end. Executive Order 10155 of August 25, 1950 is recited as background and as a non-derogation reference; it predates this order and is not an actor affecting it.\",\"independent_command_check\":\"Independent commands: the order has no command independent of the board's creation and reporting. The thirty-day report requirement and the status-quo sentence are parts of the same board-creating order and share its end; the status-quo sentence restates section 10 of the Railway Labor Act, whose force comes from that statute. The Defense Production Act compliance sentence is a condition on the board's performance, not a separate command with its own sunset shown in held evidence. The non-derogation sentence is a rule of construction preserving the Secretary of the Army's authority under EO 10155; it creates no separate duty.\",\"savings_check\":\"Savings and residual effects: EO 12553's enacting provision contains no savings or exception clause (the ACT record for this pair lists none, and the held text has none). The board's work, report and any agreement reached are completed past events rather than continuing commands of this order; the order created no right, appointment or transfer that survives independently of it, and no held source shows any continuing independent effect.\",\"scope_check\":\"Scope: EO 12553 revokes the order by number and the held table row matches its subject; the enacting provision reaches any amendments thereto and has no exception, so it reaches the whole order, not part. The order has no amendment in the held corpus. The order creates a three-member emergency board under section 10 of the Railway Labor Act (subject to that section) to investigate the dispute between certain transportation systems operated by the Secretary of the Army and certain of their employees represented by the Brotherhood of Locomotive Firemen and Enginemen, requires the board to report within thirty days from the date of the order, a sentence that nothing in the order derogates from the authority of the Secretary of the Army under Executive Order 10155 of August 25, 1950, and a sentence requiring the Board to comply with section 502 of the Defense Production Act of 1950, as amended. Inventory: the order is one operative unit; the creation of the board, its thirty-day report deadline, the non-derogation sentence and the Defense Production Act compliance sentence are all within the single whole-order clause confirmed here. Ending: Executive Order 12553 of February 25, 1986 (51 FR 7237, held government printing) revokes the numbered orders in its table, and any amendments thereto; its No. Subject table lists 10303 with a subject line matching this order (creating an emergency board to investigate a labor dispute between employees and transportation systems operated by the Secretary of the Army). The enacting provision has no stated effective date, so the revocation is read as effective on signing, 1986-02-25; the Federal Register publication date 1986-03-03 and the filing date are not effective dates. The thirty-day or stated report date and the end of any statutory status-quo period are deadlines and spent facts, not ends (owner ruling 2026-10-21 on report deadlines): the order stayed on the books until express revocation. Searches: the OFR executive-order catalog search and the USC and eCFR citation searches (coverage notes read; no coverage warning) show no instrument other than EO 12553 revoking, superseding or amending this order; the held-text citation search found citations of this number only in printings of the order itself, orders printed on the same page, Federal Register index or codification-guide material and (for some) National Mediation Board report front matter.\nMember checks: {\"earlier_end_check\":\"Candidate earlier ends examined. (1) The thirty-day report deadline is a deadline, not an end (owner ruling 2026-10-21): the order does not end when the board reports, nor when the statutory thirty-day status-quo period after the report runs out; actual report and standstill dates are spent facts that no reviewed event record in this packet supplies, so they are disclosed here rather than recorded as later events. (2) The statutory standstill in section 10 of the Railway Labor Act is the statute's own effect and not a separate command of this order. (3) No later order amends, supersedes or revokes the order before EO 12553 in the held corpus (OFR catalog and number-citation searches). EO 12553 is the first held instrument that revokes it. No earlier end is shown, so none is claimed. The sentence requiring compliance with section 502 of the Defense Production Act of 1950 is a condition on how the board performs its functions; the statute is not held in this repository, so no expiry of that statute is shown and none is claimed as an earlier end. Executive Order 10155 of August 25, 1950 is recited as background and as a non-derogation reference; it predates this order and is not an actor affecting it.\",\"independent_command_check\":\"Independent commands: the order has no command independent of the board's creation and reporting. The thirty-day report requirement and the status-quo sentence are parts of the same board-creating order and share its end; the status-quo sentence restates section 10 of the Railway Labor Act, whose force comes from that statute. The Defense Production Act compliance sentence is a condition on the board's performance, not a separate command with its own sunset shown in held evidence. The non-derogation sentence is a rule of construction preserving the Secretary of the Army's authority under EO 10155; it creates no separate duty.\",\"savings_check\":\"Savings and residual effects: EO 12553's enacting provision contains no savings or exception clause (the ACT record for this pair lists none, and the held text has none). The board's work, report and any agreement reached are completed past events rather than continuing commands of this order; the order created no right, appointment or transfer that survives independently of it, and no held source shows any continuing independent effect.\",\"scope_check\":\"Scope: EO 12553 revokes the order by number and the held table row matches its subject; the enacting provision reaches any amendments thereto and has no exception, so it reaches the whole order, not part. The order has no amendment in the held corpus. The dossier leads naming EO 10155 (and, for EO 10285, EO 10287) are resolved: EO 10155 is recited in this order's text and predates it, and EO 10287 is named only in a Federal Register codification-guide index on the same page as the held text.\"}","review_dependency_issues":[],"reviews":[{"file_sha256":"6b067cb2b9d1ca895a51a7fa83f715f8f67dde108b6d8ee7dfd009452b533611","path":"research/excavator/claude-apollo-2026-10-31/g04/packet/complete-v1/receipts/research/excavator/claude-apollo-2026-10-31/g04/packet/reviews/BLIND_REVIEW.jsonl","pointer":"line:19","review_kind":"independent_ai_source_review","scope":"whole_case","verdict":"rejected","version_binding":"exact"},{"file_sha256":"6b067cb2b9d1ca895a51a7fa83f715f8f67dde108b6d8ee7dfd009452b533611","path":"research/excavator/claude-apollo-2026-10-31/g04/packet/reviews/BLIND_REVIEW.jsonl","pointer":"line:19","review_kind":"independent_ai_source_review","scope":"whole_case","verdict":"rejected","version_binding":"exact"}],"scopes":[{"aligned_current_scopes":[],"alignment":"scope_or_source_reconciliation_required","different_current_fields":[],"scope_id":"EO:10303#whole"}],"source_pins":[{"held_text":{"expected_sha256":"6361bdc557b6acaeae6e6908cded18f4f8fef9fde1a97cc11fb7c12c8cdea761","path":"jobs/fr-originals/data/full_run/texts/EO_12553.txt","sha256":"6361bdc557b6acaeae6e6908cded18f4f8fef9fde1a97cc11fb7c12c8cdea761","state":"match"},"sha256":"945348afbba863ae7dc1fbe7df8759682fe1733962734a9255a1062c0561048c","text_path":"jobs/fr-originals/data/full_run/texts/EO_12553.txt","text_sha256":"6361bdc557b6acaeae6e6908cded18f4f8fef9fde1a97cc11fb7c12c8cdea761"},{"held_text":{"expected_sha256":"a3e1f6273aa70889f5a7bc75c1c593dd96760c2a58272c40721d894c67d7029a","path":"jobs/fr-originals/data/full_run/texts/EO_10303.txt","sha256":"a3e1f6273aa70889f5a7bc75c1c593dd96760c2a58272c40721d894c67d7029a","state":"match"},"sha256":"c04cc3d6d481631f13c57a8e5c1db3438ed4a2a5e8ca2c750299feffc138e6bd","text_path":"jobs/fr-originals/data/full_run/texts/EO_10303.txt","text_sha256":"a3e1f6273aa70889f5a7bc75c1c593dd96760c2a58272c40721d894c67d7029a"}]}]}

The order creates a three-member emergency board under section 10 of the Railway Labor Act to investigate the dispute between Pan American World Airways, Inc. and certain of its employees represented by the Transport Workers Union of America, CIO, requires the board to report within thirty days from the date of the order, and a sentence requiring the board to comply with section 502 of the Defense Production Act of 1950, as amended. Inventory: the order is one operative unit; the creation of the board, its thirty-day report deadline, the non-derogation sentence and the Defense Production Act compliance sentence and the status-quo sentence restating section 10 are all within the single whole-order clause confirmed here. Ending: Executive Order 12553 of February 25, 1986 (51 FR 7237, held government printing) revokes the numbered orders in its table, and any amendments thereto; its No. Subject table lists 10314 with a subject line matching this order (creating an emergency board to investigate a airline labor dispute). The enacting provision has no stated effective date, so the revocation is read as effective on signing, 1986-02-25; the Federal Register publication date 1986-03-03 and the filing date are not effective dates. The thirty-day or stated report date and the end of any statutory status-quo period are deadlines and spent facts, not ends (owner ruling 2026-10-21 on report deadlines): the order stayed on the books until express revocation. Searches: the OFR executive-order catalog search and the USC and eCFR citation searches (coverage notes read; no coverage warning) show no instrument other than EO 12553 revoking, superseding or amending this order; the held-text citation search found citations of this number only in printings of the order itself, orders printed on the same page, Federal Register index or codification-guide material and (for some) National Mediation Board report front matter.\nMember checks: {\"earlier_end_check\":\"Candidate earlier ends examined. (1) The thirty-day report deadline is a deadline, not an end (owner ruling 2026-10-21): the order does not end when the board reports, nor when the statutory thirty-day status-quo period after the report runs out; actual report and standstill dates are spent facts that no reviewed event record in this packet supplies, so they are disclosed here rather than recorded as later events. (2) The statutory standstill in section 10 of the Railway Labor Act is the statute's own effect and not a separate command of this order. (3) No later order amends, supersedes or revokes the order before EO 12553 in the held corpus (OFR catalog and number-citation searches). EO 12553 is the first held instrument that revokes it. No earlier end is shown, so none is claimed. The sentence requiring compliance with section 502 of the Defense Production Act of 1950 is a condition on how the board performs its functions; the statute is not held in this repository, so no expiry of that statute is shown and none is claimed as an earlier end.\",\"independent_command_check\":\"Independent commands: the order has no command independent of the board's creation and reporting. The thirty-day report requirement and the status-quo sentence are parts of the same board-creating order and share its end; the status-quo sentence restates section 10 of the Railway Labor Act, whose force comes from that statute. The Defense Production Act compliance sentence is a condition on the board's performance, not a separate command with its own sunset shown in held evidence.\",\"savings_check\":\"Savings and residual effects: EO 12553's enacting provision contains no savings or exception clause (the ACT record for this pair lists none, and the held text has none). The board's work, report and any agreement reached are completed past events rather than continuing commands of this order; the order created no right, appointment or transfer that survives independently of it, and no held source shows any continuing independent effect.\",\"scope_check\":\"Scope: EO 12553 revokes the order by number and the held table row matches its subject; the enacting provision reaches any amendments thereto and has no exception, so it reaches the whole order, not part. The order has no amendment in the held corpus.\"}","review_dependency_issues":[],"reviews":[{"file_sha256":"6b067cb2b9d1ca895a51a7fa83f715f8f67dde108b6d8ee7dfd009452b533611","path":"research/excavator/claude-apollo-2026-10-31/g04/packet/complete-v1/receipts/research/excavator/claude-apollo-2026-10-31/g04/packet/reviews/BLIND_REVIEW.jsonl","pointer":"line:20","review_kind":"independent_ai_source_review","scope":"whole_case","verdict":"rejected","version_binding":"exact"},{"file_sha256":"6b067cb2b9d1ca895a51a7fa83f715f8f67dde108b6d8ee7dfd009452b533611","path":"research/excavator/claude-apollo-2026-10-31/g04/packet/reviews/BLIND_REVIEW.jsonl","pointer":"line:20","review_kind":"independent_ai_source_review","scope":"whole_case","verdict":"rejected","version_binding":"exact"}],"scopes":[{"aligned_current_scopes":["EO:10314#whole"],"alignment":"exact_quote_and_source","different_current_fields":["state_at_cutoff","first_end","later_events"],"scope_id":"EO:10314#whole"}],"source_pins":[{"held_text":{"expected_sha256":"6361bdc557b6acaeae6e6908cded18f4f8fef9fde1a97cc11fb7c12c8cdea761","path":"jobs/fr-originals/data/full_run/texts/EO_12553.txt","sha256":"6361bdc557b6acaeae6e6908cded18f4f8fef9fde1a97cc11fb7c12c8cdea761","state":"match"},"sha256":"945348afbba863ae7dc1fbe7df8759682fe1733962734a9255a1062c0561048c","text_path":"jobs/fr-originals/data/full_run/texts/EO_12553.txt","text_sha256":"6361bdc557b6acaeae6e6908cded18f4f8fef9fde1a97cc11fb7c12c8cdea761"},{"held_text":{"expected_sha256":"c1bb5cdd850599571eadb301dafceb938a0a366eabbf658a53f1702612027cf6","path":"jobs/fr-originals/data/full_run/texts/EO_10314.txt","sha256":"c1bb5cdd850599571eadb301dafceb938a0a366eabbf658a53f1702612027cf6","state":"match"},"sha256":"15cf9643994b613a1006aa8fde4041feeb0f07bf7b778c541c8d00c723b6999d","text_path":"jobs/fr-originals/data/full_run/texts/EO_10314.txt","text_sha256":"c1bb5cdd850599571eadb301dafceb938a0a366eabbf658a53f1702612027cf6"}]}]}
